# RESULTS protoC: Sec. 10.6 method changes on the SF direction (prototype)

Generated by `python protoC.py report` from out/protoC/protoC_*.json. All dB values: more negative is better (L1 = ANR mean in dB, L2 = 10log10(sum e^2/sum d^2), ex = 10log10 of the mean Phi-excess v'Phi v/Jo over the window; div = numeric divergence, amp = L1 whole > 0 without divergence; means over non-diverged seeds).

Constants: f = 0.8500, tau_a = 3.8000, tau_m = 4.0000, h_eff = 0.2375, c = [0.24064711830131444, 0.24974721941354905, 0.24974721941354905, 0.24974721941354905, 0.010111223458038422], gz = 0.0000, gm = 0.0000, a_star = 0.1610, rho_star = 0.6707, mu_D = 0.6777, mu_fmax = 1.1278, dopt_T = 8, dopt_k = excl1, L = 4, beta_d = 0.1000, beta = 0.0500, gmin = 0.2500

Rule names: SF-Y = statistics in y coordinates, SF-E = the same rule with statistics on Delta; targets onestep / delaycap (min(onestep, a*/h_eff)) / delayopt (T-block model); correction none / g / dp / dp+g; gam = gamma of the mu_state EMA.  Second table per input: mean applied mu per phase, zero dwell of mu_state, fraction of blocks with the dp ratio clipped, the formula mu capped at mu_fmax, X_hat clipped, mu_state clipped to [0, 1.5]; PS: max excess (dB) in the 2 s after each step and time (s) from the step to excess <= -20 dB after the peak (rec) and to the 1 s pre-step mean + 3 dB (rec-rel); NaN: not reached before the next step / end of run; BU: excess folded over the 1 s burst period (seconds 5..), peak and means over phases 0-0.25 s and 0.5-1 s (dB).

## C2: tracking check

argmin T: internal step (mu_state units) minimising the mean Phi-excess over the first T updates of a constant-internal-step continuation of the actual realisation from that block, with the rule's own power correction (dp EMA carried on); grid 0 and 40 log-spaced values in [0.01, 3].  target = the rule's target at that block (NaN before the estimator starts at block 8).
